Abstract: This article studies model-free output feedback linear quadratic regulation (LQR) for continuous-time linear systems with an $n$-dimensional state, an $m$-dimensional input, and a $p$-dimensional output, using filtered input--output data. Since the system state is unavailable, existing methods rely on dynamic filters to parameterize the hidden state using measurable input--output signals. However, the intrinsic dimension of the resulting filter-based parametrization can be smaller than the dimension of the complete filtered vector, and this deterministic redundancy can make the Bellman regressions rank deficient. We characterize this intrinsic dimension and show that the conventional filtered vector contains only $2n$ independent components for single-input multi-output (SIMO) systems and $n(m+1)$ independent components for general multi-input multi-output (MIMO) systems. Based on this characterization, a reduced filtered vector is extracted directly from data and used to develop reduced model-free output feedback policy iteration and value iteration equations, eliminating the redundant directions and decreasing the number of unknown parameters while retaining a fully input--output data-based implementation. A numerical example illustrates the rank reduction and the effectiveness of the learned controller.

</details>


### [261] [Disturbance-Observer-Based Grid-Forming Control for Unbalanced Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11857)
*Juho Määttä,Marko Hinkkanen,Tuure Nurminen,Orcun Karaca,Rayane Mourouvin,Jarno Kukkola,Lennart Harnefors*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在不平衡电网电压条件下运行的构网控制方法，通过调节正序有功功率并抑制负序电压，在故障期间保持电压幅值恒定，并集成了电流限制方案，实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在电网电压不平衡条件下，传统的构网控制方法可能面临性能下降或失稳问题，需要一种能够适应不平衡故障并保持稳定运行的控制策略。

Method: 该方法仅测量交流侧电流，利用扰动观测器实现同步并提供积分和谐振作用，同时估计正负序电网电压，集成了适用于平衡和不平衡故障的电流限制方案。

Result: 通过12.5-kVA变流器实验，证明了该方法在严重平衡和不平衡故障下均能正常运行，并提供了稳定性分析和调谐指南。

Conclusion: 提出的控制方法能有效应对不平衡电网电压条件，在故障期间保持电压恒定并限制电流，具有良好的稳定性和实用性。

Abstract: This article proposes a grid-forming control method for operation under unbalanced grid-voltage conditions. The method regulates the positive-sequence active power delivered to the grid and actively suppresses the negative-sequence converter voltage, controlling the voltage magnitude to be constant also during unbalanced faults when within the physical limits of the converter. Only the converter current is measured on the AC side, and a disturbance observer is used for synchronization as well as providing integral and resonant action. Estimates for the positive- and negative-sequence grid voltage are obtained from the disturbance observer. A current-limitation scheme for both balanced and unbalanced faults is integrated. Comprehensive stability analysis and tuning guidelines are provided. Experimental results using a 12.5-kVA converter demonstrate that the proposed method can operate during severe balanced and unbalanced faults.

</details>


### [262] [Coordinated Dynamic Operation of Integrated Electrolyzer-Compressor Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11929)
*Amin Salehi,Janne Seppanen,Mahdi Pourakbari-Kasmaei*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对电解槽与电动压缩机站（EDCS）的集成系统，建立了动态模型并设计了PID控制方案，以协调应对瞬态扰动，确保系统瞬态稳定和运行可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力和氢能部门之间的互动日益频繁，电解槽和电动压缩机站的协调运行变得重要，但它们在瞬态扰动下的动态交互尚未被充分研究。

Method: 首先推导电解槽和EDCS的线性化模型以进行系统化控制器设计，然后设计两个PID控制器（保守型和快速跟踪型）来协调系统响应。

Result: 在四种不同案例下验证了模型，结果表明所提出的模型能有效应对来自压缩机驱动或电解槽的扰动，控制电解槽流量或EDCS扭矩可协调系统动态，避免不良瞬态波动或危险的超调和欠调。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架保证了集成电解槽-EDCS系统的瞬态稳定性和运行可靠性。

Abstract: The increasing interaction between power and hydrogen sectors highlights the importance of coordinated operation of electrolyzers and electric-driven compressor stations (EDCSs). This becomes particularly of higher importance under transient disturbances. However, coordinated dynamic interactions of these coupled subsystems remain largely unexplored. This article addresses such gap by developing a dynamic model for an integrated electrolyzer-EDCS system and designing appropriate PID control schemes to address the potential disturbances affecting either component. To this end, linearized models of the electrolyzer and EDCS are first derived to enable systematic controller design. Then, two PID controllers, representing conservative and fast-tracking designs, are developed to coordinate the system response. The developed coordinated model is examined and verified under four different cases. The results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed model under disturbances from the compressor driver or the electrolyzer. Controlling the electrolyzer flow in response to EDCS disturbances coordinates system dynamics and mitigates undesirable transient fluctuations. Conversely, under electrolyzer disturbances, regulating the EDCS torque eliminates inconsistent responses in pressure, flow, and rotational speed, while preventing hazardous transient undershoots and overshoots. Overall, the proposed framework guarantees transient stability and operational reliability of the integrated electrolyzer-EDCS system.

</details>


### [263] [Distributed Nash Equilibrium Seeking with Logarithmic Bit Rates over Digital Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12022)
*Zihao Ren,Chengyang Jiang,Lei Wang,Yang Liu,Kemi Ding*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 解析错误


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解析错误

Method: 解析错误

Result: 解析错误

Conclusion: 解析错误

Abstract: This paper introduces quantization techniques to reduce the communication complexity in the distributed Nash equilibrium (NE) seeking problem, achieving an exponential reduction in bit rates over digital channels. The goal of distributed NE seeking algorithms is to coordinate agents in a network game toward equilibrium through iterative message exchanges among them via a communication network. The computational complexity of this distributed algorithm critically depends on network communication overhead in the digital channel, motivating the development of communication reduction mechanism. Regarding this, we proposed some quantizers based on sparsification and uniform quantization through a general class of ultimate-boundedness-based quantizers. Based on this, we propose a Passivity-Based NE seeking Algorithm with Time-varying scaling Error state Quantization (PBA-TEQ), and show that the linear convergence can be achieved under a sufficient condition. Moreover, when employing either the scalarization quantizer or the greedy quantizer, both belonging to the ultimate-boundedness-based quantizers, within the PBA-TEQ framework, we establish a lower bound on communication complexity of $\log_2(\mathcal{O}(nd))$ bit rates per transmission to achieve unbiased linear convergence, with $n$ being the number of agents and $d$ being the dimension of the decision state of the network game. Numerical simulation examples are provided to validate our theoretical results.

</details>


### [264] [Network Topology Reconfiguration: Optimal Transition Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12047)
*Basel Morsy,Jochen Stiasny,Adolfo Anta,Jochen Cremer*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出最优过渡规划（OTP）问题，以在满足AC可行性的前提下规划网络拓扑重构的切换序列和调度轨迹，并采用滚动时域框架求解，在大型测试系统上验证了成本节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网络拓扑重构研究仅关注目标运行点，未考虑如何安全地从当前状态过渡到该目标点，导致实际执行时可能违反热极限等AC可行性约束。

Method: 建立OTP问题，联合优化切换顺序和调度轨迹，保证每个中间点满足AC潮流和热约束；使用滚动时域框架，由DC规划器生成轨迹，经AC可行性滤波器校验，通过重用组合割排除不可行拓扑。

Result: 在PGLib-OPF系统（最高1354节点）上的案例表明，该方法能生成AC可行的过渡，相比无切换ACOPF方案节省最高18.4%的运行成本。

Conclusion: 提出的方法填补了从当前状态到最优目标点之间安全过渡的空白，为实际NTR应用提供了可行性保证，并显著降低成本。

Abstract: Network topology reconfiguration (NTR) can reduce power system operating costs by co-optimizing generation dispatch and substation switching, but the reported savings describe a target operating point rather than a way to reach it. Reaching this operating point requires a sequence of intermediate operating points, each satisfying the AC power flow equations and thermal limits; because each topology admits its own feasible dispatch region, a naive transition that switches or redispatches first can drive intermediate flows past their thermal limits. Existing methods leave this gap open: snapshot NTR identifies a target but not a route. We formulate the Optimal Transition Planning (OTP) problem, co-optimizing the switching sequence and dispatch trajectory subject to AC feasibility at every intermediate point. We solve this problem with a receding-horizon framework: a DC planner proposes a trajectory that is certified against an AC feasibility filter, and infeasible topologies are excluded using reusable combinatorial cuts. Case studies on congested PGLib-OPF systems up to 1354-bus show that the method produces AC-feasible transitions that reduce operating cost by up to 18.4% compared to the no-switching ACOPF solution on commodity hardware.

</details>


### [265] [NetlistBench: Evaluating LLM Reliability in SPICE Netlist Recognition and Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12197)
*Jiarui Ma,Jianghan Wang,Yuheng Ma,Ziyi Zhuang,Xiaoguang Liu*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了NetlistBench，一个用于评估LLM在SPICE网表识别和操作任务上可靠性的基准，发现模型在简单编辑上表现出色，但在复杂结构操作上准确率显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在电路设计中的应用日益广泛，但其对面向模拟器的SPICE网表的识别和操作能力尚未得到充分研究，且常与高层设计推理混淆。

Method: 构建了包含2342个案例、24个任务族的基准NetlistBench，涵盖参数与连接识别、编辑、层级操作、等价性判断和长跨度复合编辑，并使用确定性的结构感知评估器。

Result: 六个非推理LLM的性能随操作结构复杂度变化显著：简单局部编辑准确率96%-100%，器件添加降至41%-83%，等价性判断降至49%-90%；启用推理可改善较弱模型，但未能消除结构保持失败，且随着编辑跨度增加性能急剧下降。

Conclusion: 网表可靠性是可信LLM电路设计自动化的一个独立瓶颈，需要专门关注。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used in circuit design workflows, yet their reliability on simulator-facing SPICE netlist recognition and manipulation remains poorly understood and is rarely separated from high-level design reasoning. Although netlists are textual, they encode structured circuit objects through topology and parameters. We present \textbf{NetlistBench}, a structure-verified benchmark for SPICE netlist recognition and manipulation. NetlistBench contains 2,342 cases across 24 task families, covering parameter and connectivity recognition and edits, hierarchical operations, equivalence judgment, and long-horizon compound editing. Model outputs are evaluated by a deterministic structure-aware oracle. Across six non-thinking LLMs, performance varies substantially with operation-level structural complexity. Simple local edits reach $96\%$--$100\%$ accuracy, while device addition drops to $41\%$--$83\%$ and equivalence judgment to $49\%$--$90\%$. Enabling reasoning substantially improves weaker models but does not eliminate structure-preservation failures, with performance still degrading sharply as the edit horizon increases. NetlistBench identifies netlist reliability as a distinct bottleneck for trustworthy LLM-based circuit design automation.

</details>


### [266] [SelectLight: Learning to Select Signal Plans Generated by Distributed Model Predictive Control for Urban Traffic Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12256)
*Lyuzhou Luo,Chaopeng Tan,Zhengyong Gao,Hong Zhu,Andrea D'Ariano,Keshuang Tang*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出SelectLight，一种结合多目标分布式模型预测控制和多智能体强化学习的城市交通信号控制方法，通过从DMPC生成的帕累托最优计划中选择最优方案，实现了更好的延迟性能和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DMPC方法无法从闭环结果中学习，而MARL虽能学习但可能违反信号约束，因此需要一种既能在线生成可行计划又能通过学习优化选择的方法。

Method: SelectLight使用SP-MODP生成有界非支配候选计划集，然后由基于注意力机制和IPPO的MARL策略从中选择最优计划，限制学习范围并保留约束和可解释性。

Result: 在两个28交叉口的SUMO网络上，SelectLight在延迟相关性能上最优，且在2倍需求下减少排队延迟和等待时间5.57%和6.44%，转移损失最低，计算时间远低于控制间隔。

Conclusion: SelectLight通过结合DMPC和MARL，在保持约束和可解释性的同时，显著提升了城市交通信号控制的性能，尤其在需求和场景变化下表现稳健。

Abstract: Coordinated traffic signal control across urban networks must adapt to changing demand while satisfying operational constraints. Multi-objective distributed model predictive control (DMPC) can construct feasible signal plans online, but prescribed rules for selecting among trade-off solutions cannot learn from realized closed-loop outcomes. We propose SelectLight, which implements post-optimization selection by allowing a multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) policy to choose directly from plans generated online by DMPC. At each control update, state-pruned multi-objective dynamic programming (SP-MODP) evaluates plans with a Newellian point--spatial queue model and returns a bounded set of mutually nondominated candidate signal plans for total queueing delay, peak queue accumulation, and total number of stops. A topology-aware attention policy trained with independent proximal policy optimization (IPPO) selects one unmodified plan from each variable-size set. This confines learning to candidate selection, preserves the prescribed signal timing constraints, and leaves the selected plan and its predicted objective trade-offs available for inspection. Experiments on two 28-intersection SUMO networks show that SelectLight achieves the best delay-related performance and that its advantage widens with demand. At twice the baseline demand, it reduces queueing delay and waiting time by 5.57% and 6.44%, respectively, relative to the strongest baseline. SelectLight also incurs the lowest transfer loss under every tested demand shift. With a 120 s prediction horizon, the per-intersection 99th-percentile SP-MODP solution time is 5.408 ms, well below the 5 s control interval.

</details>


### [267] [Digital Twin Networks for 6G Wireless Systems: Architecture, Enabling Technologies, Intelligent Control, and Open Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12267)
*Charalampos Oikonomidis,Emmanouel T. Michailidis,Nikolaos I. Miridakis*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文对数字孪生网络（DTN）进行了全面综述，将其架构分类为被动监控孪生和主动控制孪生，并进行了数学与计算复杂性分析，探讨了使能技术、6G用例及未解决的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G网络需要主动和确定性的编排以满足严格的性能指标，而现有文献对DTN的讨论多为概念性，缺乏技术分类和计算可行性评估，因此需要填补这一空白。

Method: 采用综述方法，对现有DTN架构进行正式分类，评估关键使能技术（如射线追踪、可重构智能表面、人工智能和移动边缘计算），并进行详细的数学和计算复杂性分析。

Result: 将DTN分为被动监控和主动控制两类，分析了其硬件可扩展性和推理瓶颈，并将其映射到智能城市、工业5.0、医疗和智能电网等6G用例，识别了GPU限制、执行延迟和零信任安全等关键挑战。

Conclusion: DTN是实现6G的关键技术，但需解决硬件、延迟和安全问题，并为实现万物互联提供了战略研究方向。

Abstract: The transition to the Sixth Generation (6G) of mobile networks requires proactive and deterministic orchestration to satisfy the stringent key performance indicators of future services, including ultra-reliable low-latency communications, enhanced mobile broadband, and massive machine-type communications. Digital Twin Networks (DTN) have recently emerged as a foundational technology to meet these demands, offering real-time and high-fidelity virtual replicas of the physical network. Although the current literature explores DTNs conceptually, a gap exists in the coverage of technical classification and computational feasibility evaluations. This survey addresses this gap by formally categorizing state-of-the-art DTN architectures into passive monitoring twins and active control twins. We provide an in-depth evaluation of their underlying enabling technologies, specifically ray-tracing, reconfigurable intelligent surfaces, artificial intelligence, and mobile edge computing. Importantly, this paper conducts a detailed mathematical and computational complexity analysis of state-of-the-art solutions to assess hardware scalability and inference bottlenecks. These architectures are then linked to various forthcoming 6G use cases, including smart cities, Industry 5.0, healthcare, and smart grids. Finally, we synthesize crucial unresolved challenges, highlighting graphics processing unit hardware limitations, cyber-physical actuation latency, and the need for a zero-trust security paradigm, offering strategic research directions to realize the unified internet of everything.

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [268] [Decodable but Not Accessible: Auditing Distance-Based Reliability Estimation on Disentangled Skin-Lesion Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11267)
*Duc-Vinh Tran*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文检验了基于距离的可靠性估计在域对抗表示学习下的假设，发现即使表示几何结构显著改变，可靠性估计性能仍低于随机水平，表明信息虽可解码但难以被非探测式可靠性估计器利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可靠性估计方法依赖表示几何反映可信度的假设，但该假设在直接重塑几何的训练干预下很少被验证。作者旨在通过域对抗学习和解缠剂量反应阶梯来审计这一假设。

Method: 使用三个检查点家族，共享相同架构和16维表示，仅在正交强度（lambda=0, 1, 5）上不同。评估多种可靠性估计器（包括马氏距离、余弦到质心、k近邻、能量分数、虚拟逻辑匹配、核密度估计器）以及监督探针，并分析几何指标与可靠性性能的关联。

Result: 表示几何随解缠强度显著变化（条件数变化两个数量级，Kendall tau=0.84），但可靠性估计AUROC在所有水平下保持平坦且低于随机（约0.40）。七个估计器结果一致，仅能量分数有孤立上升趋势。监督探针能从嵌入恢复域成员身份（AUROC 0.72-0.81），表明相关信息存在但难以被非探测估计器利用。

Conclusion: 分类性能可能掩盖表示中信息组织形式的可用性问题。信息虽可解码，但可能对基于距离的可靠性估计器不可访问，提示需要重新评估表示几何与可信度的关系。

Abstract: Distance-based reliability estimation assumes that a representation's geometry reflects its trustworthiness, yet this assumption is rarely tested under training interventions that reshape geometry directly. We audit this assumption under domain-adversarial representation learning using a disentanglement dose-response ladder. Three checkpoint families share the same architecture and a 16-dimensional representation, differing only in orthogonality strength (lambda = 0, 1, 5). Representation geometry changed substantially with disentanglement strength: the condition number shifted by two orders of magnitude (Kendall tau = 0.84, exact p = 2.8e-5). This change was not accompanied by improved reliability estimation: Mahalanobis-distance AUROC (ISIC-test vs. PAD-UFES) remained flat and below chance (about 0.40) at every level, with no significant association with any of five geometry metrics tested. The same failure was observed for cosine-to-centroid and pooled k-nearest-neighbor scorers, plus three non-distance-based scorers: an energy-based confidence score, Virtual-Logit Matching, and a kernel density estimator. Seven of eight scorers converged on the same result; the energy-based score showed an isolated upward trend that we report but do not treat as evidence against the overall pattern. A supervised probe with no access to the training objective recovered domain membership from the identical embeddings at 0.72-0.81 AUROC across every level, showing that the relevant information was not absent from the representation. These findings indicate that classification performance alone can overlook whether information in a learned representation is organized in a form that downstream reliability estimators can use. Information can remain decodable while becoming largely inaccessible to non-probing reliability estimators.

</details>


### [269] [Hardware-Aware Deployment of Joint SAR Compression and Despeckling on FPGA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11271)
*Cédric Léonard,Francescopaolo Sica,Martin Schulz*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文在ZCU102 FPGA平台上部署了联合SAR去噪和压缩（DDC）框架，通过模型适配和多种拓扑评估，发现ReLU优于GDN、残差块收益有限，且FPGA能效最高，为星载SAR压缩提供了可行方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代SAR任务产生的数据远超下行链路能力，机载数据缩减至关重要。虽有研究显示联合去噪与压缩性能优于传统方法，但未考虑星载系统的功耗、计算和操作约束。

Method: 在嵌入式ZCU102 FPGA平台上实现联合SAR去噪与压缩框架，适配定点算术和有限操作集；评估四种模型拓扑，在不同精度及CPU、GPU、FPGA平台上对比，包括替换GDN为ReLU、使用残差块等变体。

Result: ReLU替代GDN提高了SAR图像质量；残差块增加十倍计算量但收益甚微；FPGA在所测平台中能量效率最高。

Conclusion: 研究建立了可行的边缘部署工作流，并为星载SAR压缩提供了基于证据的设计起点，表明针对自然图像的压缩设计原则不一定适用于SAR图像。

Abstract: Next-generation Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR) missions will generate data far faster than they can downlink, making onboard data reduction essential for near-real-time Earth observation. Learned Image Compression (LIC) offers better rate-distortion performance than handcrafted codecs used operationally today, and recent work shows that simultaneously despeckling and compressing SAR imagery enables better representation capacity while unlocking higher compression rates. These methods, however, have yet to be confronted with the strict power, compute, and operational constraints of spaceborne systems. In this work, we bridge this gap by deploying a joint SAR Despeckling and Data Compression (DDC) framework on an embedded ZCU102 FPGA-based platform, introducing model adaptations that respect the accelerator's fixed-point arithmetic and limited set of supported operations. We evaluate four model topologies across precision levels and across CPU, GPU, and FPGA platforms, revealing several findings with direct design implications. We find that replacing conventional GDN activation functions with plain ReLU improves quality on SAR, suggesting that design principles established for compression of natural images do not necessarily transfer to SAR imagery. In addition, we demonstrate that residual blocks offer little representational benefit for ten times the compute, and show that the FPGA is the most energy-efficient of the platforms tested. Together, these results set a functioning edge deployment workflow and an evidence-based starting point for onboard SAR compression. The code is available at https://github.com/CedricLeon/SAR_DDC_FPGA.

</details>


### [270] [Geometry-Based Compression of Plenoptic Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11273)
*Davi R. Freitas,Gustavo L. Sandri,Ricardo L. de Queiroz*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Karhunen-Loève变换和多种属性编码器的全息点云属性压缩方法，可集成到MPEG G-PCC标准中，性能优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全息点云（PPC）通过多视角颜色提供更真实的表现，但其属性数据量巨大，需要高效的压缩方法。

Method: 先对颜色属性进行Karhunen-Loève变换去相关，再利用多种带帧内预测的属性编码器进行压缩，可嵌入G-PCC标准。

Result: 在不同空间分辨率的PPC上，压缩性能优于RAHT和基于视频的PCC方案，成为新的最先进方法。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效压缩PPC属性，且兼容现有标准，有望成为PPC压缩的新基准。

Abstract: Plenoptic point clouds (PPC) are novel data structures that represent the light from different viewing directions in order to provide a higher degree of realism to regular point clouds. This is achieved by associating each point to multiple colors instead of a single one. Here, we present a method to efficiently compress the attributes of a PPC, consisting of a Karhunen-Loève transform over the color attributes followed by multiple attribute coders with intra prediction capability. This compression scheme can be incorporated within the MPEG's geometry-based PCC (G-PCC) standard, using any of G-PCC's existing solutions for attribute coding. Compression performance assessment using PPCs of different spatial resolutions reveals competitive results in comparison to existing methods, such as RAHT-based or video-based PCC solutions. We believe our coder to be the new state of the art.

</details>


### [271] [Uncertainty-Aware and Explainable Ensemble Deep Learning Framework for Multi-Class Skin Lesion Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11280)
*Rofiqul Islam,Lilatul Ferdouse*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出一种结合视觉Transformer和CNN的深度集成学习框架，通过MC Dropout和Grad-CAM++实现不确定性估计与可解释性，在HAM10000上达到96%准确率和99% ROC-AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 皮肤癌诊断面临类内变异大、类间相似性高、类别不平衡及深度学习模型可解释性差等挑战，需要更可靠且可解释的诊断工具。

Method: 采用MaxViT-Tiny、ConvNeXt-Tiny和EfficientNetV2-B0进行深度集成，使用MC Dropout估计预测不确定性，并通过Grad-CAM++提供视觉解释。

Result: 在HAM10000数据集上，经不确定性过滤（熵<1.0，置信度≥0.7）后，准确率96%，ROC-AUC 99%，宏平均精确率、召回率、F1分数分别为94%、95%、95%，加权平均均为96%。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了准确、可解释且具有不确定性意识的皮肤病变分类，有助于可信的计算机辅助诊断。

Abstract: Skin cancer diagnosis from dermoscopic images remains challenging due to high intra-class variability, inter-class similarity, class imbalance, and the limited interpretability of deep learning models. This paper proposes an uncertainty-aware and explainable deep learning framework for multi-class skin lesion classification. The framework combines a vision transformer model (MaxViT-Tiny) with CNN-based models (ConvNeXt-Tiny and EfficientNetV2-B0) through deep ensemble learning. Monte Carlo (MC) Dropout estimates predictive uncertainty and identifies unreliable predictions, while Grad-CAM++, an explainable AI (XAI) technique, provides visual explanations by highlighting lesion regions that influence model decisions. Evaluated on the HAM10000 dataset, the framework achieves 96% accuracy and 99% ROC-AUC under uncertainty-aware filtering (entropy < 1.0, confidence >= 0.7), with macro-average precision, recall, and F1-score of 94%, 95%, and 95%, respectively, and 96% weighted-average scores across all three metrics. The results demonstrate accurate, interpretable, and uncertainty-aware skin lesion classification for trustworthy computer-aided diagnosis.

</details>


### [272] [Physics-Informed Implicit Neural Representations for Improved Myocardial Perfusion MRI Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11282)
*Christos Tsepas,Chang Yan,Maximilian Fuetterer,Sebastian Kozerke,Cian M Scannell*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出将时空隐式神经表示（INR）与物理信息神经网络（PINN）结合，用于定量心肌灌注CMR参数估计，在仿真数据上提高了精度和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多室交换模型拟合对噪声和采集变异性敏感，而先前PINN方法在定量灌注CMR中虽有潜力，但仍需提升参数估计的准确性和平滑性。

Method: 扩展了现有PINN框架，引入时空隐式神经表示（INR）将MR信号表示为连续时空函数，从而增强模型对信号时空相关性的利用。

Result: 在仿真CMR数据集上，所提方法较已有方法在参数估计准确性和鲁棒性上均有所提升。

Conclusion: PINN结合时空INR能有效改善定量灌注CMR的参数估计性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Quantifying myocardial perfusion from cardiac magnetic resonance (CMR) can be achieved by fitting tracer-kinetic models to the dynamic contrast-enhanced MR data. However, fitting the observed data with multi-compartment exchange models, which describe the evolution of the contrast agent in the tissue, to estimate perfusion parameters is a challenging inverse problem that is sensitive to noise and acquisition variability. Previously, physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have been proposed as an alternative to conventional non-linear least squares fitting methods with promising results for quantitative perfusion CMR. In this work, we extend the previously proposed PINN framework with spatiotemporal implicit neural representations (INRs) to represent the MR signal as a continuous spatiotemporal function and to improve the accuracy, smoothness, and physical consistency of the PINN model. In realistic simulated CMR datasets, our proposed PINN with INRs demonstrates improved robustness and parameter estimation accuracy over the previously established methods. The code is available at https://github.com/q-cardIA/pinn-inr.

</details>


### [273] [Uncertainty-Aware Compositional Localization and Placement Assessment of Catheters and Tubes in Chest X-Rays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11288)
*Harshil Lodhiya*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: UCompCXR 是一种组合式框架，通过检测导管片段、图聚类关联、精度加权融合尖端预测，实现胸部X光片上每个设备的放置分类，相比基线检测出更多设备并减少误报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习方法要么全局分类丢失设备位置信息，要么将所有设备分割为单一掩码，无法在导管重叠时进行逐设备评估，而导管放置评估对安全至关重要。

Method: UCompCXR：先检测局部导管片段，通过基于图的聚类将片段关联为设备实例，再用精度加权高斯估计融合各片段的尖端预测，最后对每个设备进行分类。

Result: 在RANZCR CLiP数据集上，UCompCXR比强多任务基线多检测26%的设备，误报减少75%，尖端不确定性校准良好（95%覆盖率=0.948）。在匹配设备上，灾难性定位失败大幅减少，模型仅2.27M参数，可部署于资源受限硬件。

Conclusion: UCompCXR能有效提升导管放置评估的准确性和可解释性，尤其对鼻胃管等设备，且模型轻量，适合临床部署。

Abstract: Assessing catheter and tube placement on chest X-rays is safety-critical yet tedious and error-prone. Current deep learning methods either classify placement globally -- losing track of which device is where -- or segment all devices into a single mask, making per-device assessment impossible when catheters overlap. We introduce UCompCXR, a compositional framework that detects local catheter fragments, associates them into device instances via graph-based clustering, fuses per-fragment tip predictions through precision-weighted Gaussian estimation, and classifies placement per device. On the RANZCR CLiP dataset (30,083 images, 5-fold patient-level CV with bootstrap CIs), UCompCXR detects 26% more devices than a strong multi-task baseline sharing the same MobileNetV3 backbone, with 75% fewer false positives and well-calibrated tip uncertainty (95% coverage = 0.948). The aggregate tip error rises -- but only because the model finds devices the baseline misses entirely, especially nasogastric tubes. On matched devices, catastrophic localization failures drop substantially. At 2.27M parameters in a single forward pass, the model is deployable on resource-constrained clinical hardware.

</details>


### [274] [SinoDiff: Physics-Consistent Self-Supervised Diffusion for Unified Low-Dose to Standard-Dose PET Sinogram Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11514)
*Ghulam Nabi Ahmad Hassan Yar,Himashi Peiris,Sharna Jamadar,Alex Fornito,Zhaolin Chen*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出了一种自监督、物理一致的扩散框架SinoDiff，通过将PET采集模型集成到前向扩散过程中，实现多剂量水平下PET正弦图的恢复，无需重新训练，性能优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LD-PET恢复方法存在泛化性差、依赖配对数据、自监督方法质量不佳等问题，限制了实际应用。

Method: SinoDiff采用Poisson thinning将PET采集模型融入扩散前向过程，反向过程估计信号增量，并结合频域卷积捕捉正弦图的长程依赖。

Result: 在[18F]-FDG和[18F]-FDOPA数据集上，SinoDiff在多剂量水平下达到与监督和自监督基线相当或更优的性能。

Conclusion: SinoDiff是一种统一、无需重训练的自监督方法，能有效恢复多剂量水平的PET图像，提高图像质量并保留病理细节。

Abstract: Low-dose positron emission tomography (LD-PET) reduces radiation exposure but leads to poor image quality and hinders diagnostic confidence. Existing supervised LD to standard-dose (SD) PET recovery methods often fail to generalise across dose variations, while dose-agnostic supervised methods require paired LD-SD data. Current self-supervised methods, although more flexible, typically produce inferior results, including loss of anatomical details and oversmoothing pathological features. These pose major limitations for practical applications. To overcome these limitations, we propose SinoDiff, a novel self-supervised, physics-consistent diffusion framework for the recovery of PET sinograms across multiple predefined dose levels. Unlike the noise simulation in traditional diffusion methods, SinoDiff integrates the PET acquisition model into the forward diffusion process via Poisson thinning, enabling physically consistent sampling/modelling of dose-dependent count statistics. During the reverse diffusion process, SinoDiff estimates the incremental change in PET signals from predefined dose levels. Therefore, SinoDiff is a single, unified model that requires no retraining across multiple dose levels. To consider the characteristics of PET sinogram, we incorporate a frequency-domain convolution to capture long-range dependencies across projection angles and detector bins. Experiments on [18F]-FDG and [18F]-FDOPA datasets demonstrate that SinoDiff achieves competitive performance against supervised and self-supervised baselines across multiple dose levels.

</details>


### [275] [Boundary-Continuous Cross-Camera RGB Mapping via Hue-Split Model Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11548)
*Yuma Kinoshita,Hitoshi Kiya*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出一种色调分割模型树方法，用于跨相机的RGB颜色映射，通过递归分割色调空间并融合节点颜色校正矩阵，在提高精度的同时避免硬分割带来的假轮廓。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局仿射颜色校正矩阵无法捕捉相机间色调特定的差异，导致跨相机颜色映射不准确。

Method: 沿标量色调坐标递归分割源相机颜色空间，构建存储仿射CCM的模型树；使用对数域误差拟合节点CCM；引入边界连续公式，通过混合根到叶路径上所有节点CCM的对数域输出来预测，并用单纯形约束优化路径混合权重，同时结合图表对拟合损失和边界原型对的正则化项。

Result: 在Canon EOS-1Ds Mark II到Canon EOS 20D的映射实验中，色调分割显著降低了log-RMSE，所提路径混合与边界原型正则化在两种光源和多种曝光条件下同时提高了精度并减少了色度间隙。

Conclusion: 所提方法能有效处理色调特定差异，在提高跨相机颜色映射精度的同时避免硬分割伪影，优于全局仿射CCM。

Abstract: We propose a hue-split model-tree method for boundary-continuous cross-camera RGB mapping. Cross-camera RGB mapping aims to produce consistent color representations across cameras whose recorded RGB values differ due to sensor spectral sensitivities and image-signal processing pipelines. A common chart-based remedy is to estimate a single global affine color correction matrix (CCM), but such a global model cannot capture hue-specific discrepancies between cameras. To capture that behavior, we recursively partitions the source-camera color space along a scalar hue coordinate and builds an model tree that stores an affine CCM at every node. For fitting the node CCMs, we utilize a log-domain error objective. To prevent false contours that arise from hard hue splits, we further introduce a boundary-continuous formulation in which the prediction is obtained by blending the log-domain outputs of all node CCMs along the root-to-leaf path. The path-wise blending weights are optimized under a simplex constraint using both a chart-pair fitting loss and an explicit continuity regularizer defined on deterministic boundary prototype pairs placed just on either side of each learned hue threshold. We conducted an experiment on a Canon EOS-1Ds Mark II to Canon EOS 20D mapping using the Middlebury Registered Color Checker dataset. The results show that hue splitting substantially reduces log-RMSE over a single global affine CCM and that the proposed path blending with boundary prototype regularization simultaneously improves accuracy and suppresses chromaticity gaps at the learned hue thresholds across two illuminants and multiple exposure conditions.

</details>


### [276] [Alignment of Similarity-Transformed Images Based on Fourier--Mellin Transform Using Auxiliary Function Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11565)
*Shinji Yamashita,Yuma Kinoshita,Hitoshi Kiya*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于相位相关的图像相似变换估计方法，结合对数极坐标表示和辅助函数法，实现了亚像素精度的尺度、旋转和平移估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 傅里叶-梅林配准方法在需要亚像素精度时往往无法达到足够的对齐精度，因此需要一种更精确的估计方法。

Method: 该方法结合了对数极坐标下傅里叶幅度谱的尺度与旋转估计，以及基于辅助函数法的相位相关最大化，采用两阶段流程：先估计尺度与旋转，再通过校正图像对在空间域以亚像素精度估计平移。

Result: 仿真实验表明，与基于离散互相关的傅里叶-梅林配准方法相比，该方法在尺度、旋转和平移的估计误差上均有降低。

Conclusion: 该方法能够更准确地估计图像间的相似变换，实现亚像素级配准精度。

Abstract: This paper proposes an algorithm for estimating the similarity transformation, namely translation, scale, and rotation, between two images with subpixel accuracy. Image registration is a fundamental technique for aligning images acquired under different viewpoints and imaging conditions, and a representative approach based on maximizing discrete cross-correlation is the Fourier--Mellin registration. However, the Fourier--Mellin approach often fails to achieve sufficient alignment accuracy when subpixel-level estimation is required. The proposed method integrates (i) scale-and-rotation estimation from the Fourier magnitude spectrum in a log-polar representation and (ii) maximization of phase-only correlation based on the auxiliary function method. This integration enables a two-stage estimation procedure: it first estimates scale and rotation without being affected by translation, and then estimates translation with subpixel precision in the spatial domain using the corrected image pair. A simulation experiment on image pairs subjected to random similarity transformations demonstrates that the proposed method reduces estimation errors in scale, rotation, and translation compared with Fourier--Mellin-based registration methods using discrete cross-correlation.

</details>


### [277] [A comparison of CNN architectures for Alzheimer's disease detection in single-view MRI scans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11762)
*Hiram Zuniga,Ulises Orozco-Rosas,Kenia Picos*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基准测试，评估十种CNN架构在阿尔茨海默病MRI分类上的性能，使用OASIS数据集和两阶段迁移学习，发现VGG16最佳，且所有模型在区分非痴呆和极轻度痴呆时均存在困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿尔茨海默病无法治愈，早期检测至关重要，而深度学习适用于基于MRI的诊断，但现有研究缺乏统一的评估协议，需要公平比较不同架构。

Method: 采用两阶段迁移学习和全微调流程，在OASIS数据集的3,900张类平衡子集上训练十种CNN（ResNet、DenseNet、MobileNet、EfficientNet、VGG），并在相同留出测试集上评估。

Result: VGG16获得最佳性能，验证准确率0.9637，测试准确率0.9533；所有架构在非痴呆到极轻度痴呆的过渡分类上表现不佳。

Conclusion: CNN在阿尔茨海默病分类中有效，但早期阶段（特别是非痴呆到极轻度）的识别仍是挑战，未来需改进特征提取或数据增强。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease is a leading cause of death with no cure. Therefore, early detection is critical to slow progression and preserve quality of life. Diagnosis relies on medical history, cognitive tests, physical exams, and MRI brain scans, making deep learning suitable for Alzheimer's classification. This work proposes a benchmark that evaluates ten different convolutional neural network (CNN) architectures (including ResNet, DenseNet, MobileNet, EfficientNet, and VGG family models) under the same held-out test split protocol. A two-stage transfer learning and full fine-tuning pipeline is introduced to perform training using a class-balanced subset (3,900 images) derived from the OASIS medical imaging dataset, comprising 86,437 single-view MRI brain scans labeled into four classifications of Alzheimer's disease: Non-Demented, Very Mild Dementia, Mild Dementia, and Moderate Dementia. The best results were achieved by VGG16, with a 0.9637 validation accuracy and a 0.9533 test accuracy score. A key finding documented in this work is the difficulty of classifying the transition from Non-Demented to Very Mild Demented stages, observed consistently across all ten architectures.

</details>


### [278] [ResPCC: A Loss-Resilient Neural Point Cloud Codec over Lossy Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11845)
*Xueqin Niu,Mufan Liu,Yifan Wang,Le Yang,Jun Sun,Yiling Xu*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: ResPCC是首个端到端、对数据丢失具有内在鲁棒性的神经点云编解码器，通过损失率感知调制、空间通道交织和掩码感知恢复，在5%-30%丢包率下优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型点云压缩方法假设理想传输条件，实际网络中的丢包会导致潜在特征失真、坐标漂移和几何退化，缺乏针对数据丢失的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出ResPCC框架，包含编码端的条件自适应潜在调制（CALM）和空间通道交织（SCI），以及解码端的掩码感知图潜在恢复（MGLR）和字典细化（DBR）。

Result: 在ShapeNet和SemanticKITTI数据集上，5%-30%丢包率下，ResPCC比基线方法具有更好的稳定性和率失真性能，重建保真度高。

Conclusion: ResPCC为实际网络中的3D数据传输提供了可靠解决方案，是首个内在抗丢包的神经点云编解码器，代码已开源。

Abstract: Point cloud compression (PCC) is critical for efficient storage and transmission of 3D data. While recent learning-based PCC methods achieve good rate-distortion (R-D) performance, they generally rely on ideal transmission conditions. In practice, packet loss is a common issue and can severely distort latent features, causing coordinate drift and geometric degradation. To address this challenge, we present ResPCC, the first end-to-end neural point cloud codec designed to offer intrinsic resilience against data loss. Our framework is loss-rate-aware and adapts to diverse packet loss conditions. At the encoder, we introduce a Condition-Adaptive Latent Modulation (CALM) module to adjust latent feature distributions according to the perceived loss rate, as well as a Spatial-Channel Interleaving (SCI) mechanism that transforms channel-wise data extinction into spatially scattered element-wise missing patterns. At the decoder, we develop a Mask-Aware Graph-based Latent Restoration (MGLR) module, followed by a Dictionary-based Refinement (DBR) stage to recover corrupted features and align them with canonical priors. Evaluations on ShapeNet and SemanticKITTI under 5\% to 30\% packet loss rates show that ResPCC consistently delivers superior stability and R-D performance over baselines. Our framework maintains high reconstruction fidelity under lossy conditions, providing a reliable solution for 3D data transmission over practical networks. Code is available at https://github.com/starrynight314/ResPCC.

</details>


### [279] [Domain-Aware Lightweight Spectral-Grouped Convolutions for Hyperspectral Fish Freshness Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12227)
*Kazi Nabiul Alam,Pooneh Bagheri Zadeh,Akbar Sheikh-Akbari*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出轻量级网络SGNet，通过分组卷积、深度可分离空间路径和双注意力机制，有效利用高光谱数据的谱间与空间特征，实现三文鱼新鲜度的高精度预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统深度学习方法未充分考虑高光谱数据的特性，如谱信息主导、标签有序以及训练样本少，导致性能受限。

Method: SGNet结构：分组卷积提取谱特征，深度可分离卷积处理空间特征，结合通道注意力与空间门控的双注意力机制，以及轻量化设计。

Result: SGNet在自建三文鱼数据集上达到97.8%的分类准确率和0.64天的MAE，仅需4.75M参数，比ResNet-50和ViT减少5到18倍参数量。

Conclusion: 领域感知的网络设计能够实现精确、实时的鱼类新鲜度预测，适合工业部署。

Abstract: Hyperspectral imaging (HSI) offers nondestructive assessment of fish freshness by detecting biochemical alterations across spectral bands. However, conventional deep learning approaches do not fully address the particular characteristics of HSI data, such as spectral dominance over spatial textures, ordinal label structure, and a small number of training samples. We propose SGNet (Spectral-Grouped Network), a lightweight architecture that separates spectral and spatial feature extraction using grouped convolutions and a depthwise spatial pathway. A dual attention mechanism that couples channel-wise squeeze-and-excitation with spatial gating adaptively highlights informative features. SGNet achieves 97.8% classification accuracy and 0.64 days mean absolute error (MAE) with just 4.75M parameters when tested on our newly developed 16-day refrigerator-stored salmon fillet dataset. Ablation studies validate the contribution of each component, while comparisons demonstrate a five- to eighteen-fold parameter reduction relative to ResNet-50 and Vision Transformers. Our findings indicate that domain-aware design supports precise, real-time freshness prediction for industrial implementation.

</details>
